Full-suite regression found and fixed (3.10, at 12543a8): test_out_of_core_relation.py::
test_child_fk_join_fractional_float_parent_matches_fractional_decimal_child[REMAP] failed because
the shared REMAP mint typed every input through the joiners' up-front rule, refusing a decimal child
on the whole-child path that accepts it (class 4, route parity). The mint now types only tz-naive
timestamps and keeps inference otherwise (byte-identical to main for non-temporal keys).

==========================================================================================
Round 2 (argon build review of 6bb18850: GO-WITH-FIXES, 1 HIGH, 1 MEDIUM, 1 LOW)
==========================================================================================
Environment: uv.lock (= origin/main 0090f6f9's lock) synced with uv sync --frozen, extras dev lint
vault cloud + the lock's xdist extra; Python 3.10.20 and 3.12.3; pyarrow 24.0.0, duckdb 1.5.4;
native companion not installed. Cells run with -n 4. "6bb18850" = round-1 head with this round's
tests over it; "after" = 23172bf9.

Dispositions
- HIGH (_identity_temporal_promotion exempts s|ms|us -> ns): REJECTED. The ns fixed type comes from
  the child side; under an identity parent every published child value is a child instant
  (PRESERVE/WARN orphan, REMAP minted from the child keys by mint_remap_values) or a parent instant
  equal to one on the exact epoch-ns token (_fk_keys._epoch_nanoseconds, unbounded int), so no
  parent key outside the ns range reaches the ns column. Evidence: cells at 0001 and 9999 and at
  each ns-range edge plus one parent-unit step outside, every policy x gate x mode, equal
  full-frame and stay out_of_core; an unseeded parent key is refused by the out-of-core runner
  itself (out_of_core_parent_seed_missing) and routed sequential by the pipeline, so its exemption
  arm decides only a verdict. Comment restated as that invariant; no code change to the router.
- MEDIUM (mask_child_fk output_types=None): test oracle only. Static: no src module calls it
  (definition, mask_child_fk_fail wrapper, package re-export; no caller in decoy, decoy-platform,
  decoy-web). Dynamic: test_no_production_route_reaches_the_whole_child_oracle_R2CH patches it to
  raise on every gate x mode + full_frame and all runs complete. Its value inference (s|ms|ns ->
  us, ns keys 1 ns apart collapse) is pinned as characterized, not guaranteed.
- LOW (fk_output_array lets the cast error ride as __cause__): REJECTED as written (its coded error
  has no cause, context or value; pinned R2CH). The class is real one call away: cast_fk_chunk
  raised pyarrow's cast error, which quotes the key value, raw to _concat_fk_chunks and
  _batch_join._cast_chunks. Fixed: same Arrow class re-raised with types only, outside the except
  (the pinned whole-column parity battery expects the Arrow family). Neighbour fixed: an
  unimplemented cast escaped fk_output_array as a raw ArrowNotImplementedError; it now catches
  every Arrow error into the one coded refusal.

Survivors, both equivalent as reconstructed (round 1 recorded them red with its own script, which
cannot be reproduced; that record is superseded):
- R1-M5 (REMAP input typed for every key type): for a non-temporal target fk_output_array validates
  the cast but returns the inferred array, and fk_key_value-normalized child keys always cast to their
  own source type, so outputs are identical.
- R1-M7b (safe=False on temporal construction): _holds_exactly compares every value with the built
  array and refuses truncation and wraparound alike, so the two guards are redundant by design and no
  behavioral test separates them (R1-M7a, which drops the exactness check, is red).

admission, append before the typed build, NaT in the token); the corrected three plus R3-1/R3-2 rerun
alone at 49b2828f: 5/5 red.
